==================================================
START FILE: .\modules\telegram\telegram.service.ts
==================================================

import axios from 'axios';
import { supabase } from '../../config/supabase';
import { pool, withTransactionAs } from '../../config/db';
import { ScheduleRepository } from '../schedule/schedule.repository';
import { ProfileRepository } from '../technician/profile.repository';
import { sendTelegramNotification } from '../../services/telegramService';
import { broadcastCalendarUpdate } from '../../services/realtimeService';
import { SERVICE_TYPE_MAP, getServiceTypeKeys } from '../../services/scheduleService';
import { logger } from '../../utils/logger';
import { ScheduleStatus } from '../../constants/enums';

export class TelegramService {
    public botUsername: string = '';

    constructor(
        private scheduleRepo: ScheduleRepository,
        private profileRepo: ProfileRepository
    ) {}

    async getBotInfo() {
        return { username: this.botUsername };
    }

    async setWebhook(url: string) {
        const token = process.env.TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN;
        const secretToken = process.env.TELEGRAM_WEBHOOK_SECRET;
        if (!url || !token) throw new Error('URL and token are required');

        const webhookUrl = `${url}/api/telegram/webhook`;
        await axios.post(`https://api.telegram.org/bot${token}/setWebhook`, { 
            url: webhookUrl,
            secret_token: secretToken
        });
        return { message: `Webhook set to ${webhookUrl}` };
    }

    async processTelegramUpdate(update: any) {
        const token = process.env.TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN;
        if (!token) return;

        if (update.message) logger.debug('[TELEGRAM] Received message');
        if (update.callback_query) logger.debug('[TELEGRAM] Received callback_query');

        if (update.message?.text?.startsWith('/start ')) {
            const profileId = update.message.text.split(' ')[1];
            const uuidRegex = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i;
            if (uuidRegex.test(profileId)) {
                logger.info({ profileId, chat_id: update.message.chat.id }, '[TELEGRAM] Associating profile');
                await withTransactionAs(profileId, async (db) => {
                    await db.query('UPDATE profiles SET telegramchatid = $1 WHERE id = $2', [update.message.chat.id.toString(), profileId]);
                });
                await sendTelegramNotification('✅ Conta associada com sucesso!', update.message.chat.id.toString());
            }
        }

        if (update.callback_query) {
            const callbackQueryId = update.callback_query.id;
            const callbackData = update.callback_query.data;
            const chatIdUnsafe = update.callback_query.message.chat.id;
            const chatId = String(chatIdUnsafe);
            const messageId = update.callback_query.message.message_id;

            if (callbackData.startsWith('sch_acc_') || callbackData.startsWith('sch_rej_')) {
                logger.info({ callbackData, chatId, messageId }, '[TELEGRAM] Button clicked');

                const isAccept = callbackData.startsWith('sch_acc_');
                const scheduleIdStr = isAccept ? callbackData.replace('sch_acc_', '') : callbackData.replace('sch_rej_', '');
                const scheduleId = Number(scheduleIdStr);
                const newState = isAccept ? ScheduleStatus.ACCEPTED : ScheduleStatus.REJECTED;

                if (isNaN(scheduleId)) {
                    logger.error({ callbackData }, '[TELEGRAM] Invalid schedule ID in callback');
                    return;
                }

                await axios.post(`https://api.telegram.org/bot${token}/answerCallbackQuery`, {
                    callback_query_id: callbackQueryId,
                    text: isAccept ? 'A aceitar agendamento...' : 'A rejeitar agendamento...'
                }).catch(err => logger.error(err.message, '[TELEGRAM] Erro ao responder callback query:'));

                let technicianId: string | null = null;
                try {
                    // Descobrimos o id do tecnico antes da transação principal (ou lá dentro) via profileRepo
                    // Precisamos de pool para usar findByTelegramChatId, vamos injecta-lo
                    const profile = await this.profileRepo.findByTelegramChatId(chatId, pool);
                    technicianId = profile?.id || null;

                    await withTransactionAs(technicianId || 'telegram-webhook', async (db) => {
                        const { rowCount } = await db.query(
                            'UPDATE schedules SET "acknowledgementState" = $1 WHERE id = $2',
                            [newState, scheduleId]
                        );

                        if (rowCount === 0) {
                            throw new Error(`Agendamento ${scheduleId} não encontrado.`);
                        }

                        logger.info({ scheduleId, newState, technicianId }, '[TELEGRAM] Schedule status updated');
                    });

                    broadcastCalendarUpdate(supabase, scheduleId);

                    const s = await this.scheduleRepo.findById(scheduleId);
     
                    if (!s) {
                        logger.error(`[TELEGRAM] Schedule ${scheduleId} not found for message update`);
                        return;
                    }
     
                    const clientName = s.clientName || 'Cliente';
                    const startDate = s.startDate ? new Date(s.startDate).toLocaleString('pt-PT', { timeZone: 'Europe/Lisbon' }) : 'A definir';
                    const endDate = s.endDate ? new Date(s.endDate).toLocaleString('pt-PT', { timeZone: 'Europe/Lisbon' }) : 'A definir';
                    const serviceTypeKeys = getServiceTypeKeys(s.serviceType);
                    const serviceType = serviceTypeKeys.length > 0 ? SERVICE_TYPE_MAP[serviceTypeKeys[0]] || serviceTypeKeys[0] : 'Não especificado';
     
                    const equipmentInfo = `${s.equipmentBrand || ''} ${s.equipmentModel || ''}${s.equipmentSerialNumber ? ` (S/N: ${s.equipmentSerialNumber})` : ''}`.trim() || 'Não especificado';

                    const statusEmoji = isAccept ? '✅' : '❌';
                    const statusText = isAccept ? 'ACEITE' : 'REJEITADO';

                    let updatedMsg = `${statusEmoji} <b>AGENDAMENTO ${statusText}</b>\n\n`;
                    updatedMsg += `<b>Tipo de Serviço:</b> ${serviceType}\n`;
                    updatedMsg += `<b>Cliente:</b> ${clientName}\n`;
                    updatedMsg += `<b>Equipamento:</b> ${equipmentInfo}\n`;
                    updatedMsg += `<b>Início:</b> ${startDate}\n`;
                    updatedMsg += `<b>Final:</b> ${endDate}\n`;

                    if (s.additionalInfo) updatedMsg += `<b>Notas Internas:</b> ${s.additionalInfo}\n`;

                    if (s.parts && s.parts.length > 0) {
                        updatedMsg += `\n<b>Peças Necessárias:</b>\n`;
                        s.parts.forEach((p: any) => {
                            updatedMsg += `• ${p.quantity}x ${p.reference} - ${p.designation}\n`;
                        });
                    }

                    updatedMsg += isAccept ? `\nObrigado por confirmar o agendamento! Bom trabalho!` : `\nO agendamento foi rejeitado.`;

                    await axios.post(`https://api.telegram.org/bot${token}/editMessageText`, {
                        chat_id: chatId,
                        message_id: messageId,
                        text: updatedMsg,
                        parse_mode: 'HTML'
                    }).catch(err => logger.error(err.message, '[TELEGRAM] Erro ao editar mensagem:'));

                } catch (err: any) {
                    logger.error(err, '[TELEGRAM] Error in callback query processing:');
                    await axios.post(`https://api.telegram.org/bot${token}/sendMessage`, {
                        chat_id: chatId,
                        text: `❌ Erro ao processar: ${err.message}`
                    }).catch(() => { });
                }
            }
        }
    }

    async syncTelegramUpdates() {
        const token = process.env.TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN;
        if (!token) return { success: false, error: 'No token' };
        try {
            const { data } = await axios.get(`https://api.telegram.org/bot${token}/getUpdates`);
            for (const update of data.result) {
                await this.processTelegramUpdate(update);
            }
            return { success: true, count: data.result.length };
        } catch (err: any) {
            return { success: false, error: err.message };
        }
    }

    async initializeTelegramBot() {
        const token = process.env.TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN;
        const url = process.env.TELEGRAM_WEBHOOK_URL;

        if (!token) {
            logger.warn('[TELEGRAM] No bot token found. Telegram features disabled.');
            return;
        }

        try {
            const { data: me } = await axios.get(`https://api.telegram.org/bot${token}/getMe`);
            this.botUsername = me.result.username;
            logger.info(`[TELEGRAM] Bot identified: @${this.botUsername}`);

            if (url) {
                const webhookUrl = `${url}/api/telegram/webhook`;
                const secretToken = process.env.TELEGRAM_WEBHOOK_SECRET;
                await axios.post(`https://api.telegram.org/bot${token}/setWebhook`, { 
                    url: webhookUrl,
                    secret_token: secretToken
                });
                logger.info(`[TELEGRAM] Webhook auto-configured to: ${webhookUrl}${secretToken ? ' (with Secret Token)' : ''}`);
            }
        } catch (err) {
            logger.error(err, '[TELEGRAM] Initialization failed:');
        }
    }
}

START FILE: .\modules\ticket\ticket.controller.ts

import { Response } from 'express';
import { AuthenticatedRequest } from '../../middlewares/auth.middleware';
import { catchAsync } from '../../utils/catchAsync';
import { BadRequestError, UnauthorizedError } from '../../utils/ApiError';
import { TicketService } from './ticket.service';

export class TicketController {
    constructor(private ticketService: TicketService) {}

    getTickets = catchAsync(async (req: AuthenticatedRequest, res: Response) => {
        const status = (req.query.status as string) || 'open';
        const page  = Math.max(1, Number(req.query.page) || 1);
        const limit = Math.min(1000, Math.max(1, Number(req.query.limit) || 100));

        const { data: tickets, total } = await this.ticketService.getTickets({ status, page, limit });
        const result = tickets.map((t: any) => ({ ...t, faultDescription: t.faultDescription || t.fault_description }));

        res.json({ data: result, pagination: { page, limit, total, totalPages: Math.ceil(total / limit) } });
    });

    getTicketById = catchAsync(async (req: AuthenticatedRequest, res: Response) => {
        const ticketId = Number(req.params.id);
        if (!ticketId || Number.isNaN(ticketId)) throw new BadRequestError('Invalid ticket id');
        const ticket = await this.ticketService.getTicketById(ticketId);
        res.json(ticket);
    });

    createTicket = catchAsync(async (req: AuthenticatedRequest, res: Response) => {
        if (!req.user) throw new UnauthorizedError();
        const result = await this.ticketService.createTicket(req.body, req.user.id);
        res.status(201).json(result);
    });

    replyToTicket = catchAsync(async (req: AuthenticatedRequest, res: Response) => {
        if (!req.user) throw new UnauthorizedError();
        const result = await this.ticketService.replyToTicket(+req.params.id, req.body.message, req.user.id);
        res.json(result);
    });

    deleteTicket = catchAsync(async (req: AuthenticatedRequest, res: Response) => {
        const ticketId = Number(req.params.id);
        if (!ticketId || Number.isNaN(ticketId)) throw new BadRequestError('Invalid ticket id');
        const result = await this.ticketService.deleteTicket(ticketId, req.user!.id);
        res.json(result);
    });


    markTicketAsRead = catchAsync(async (req: AuthenticatedRequest, res: Response) => {
        if (!req.user) throw new UnauthorizedError();
        await this.ticketService.markTicketAsRead(+req.params.id, req.user.id);
        res.status(200).send('Marked as read');
    });

    linkTicketToSchedule = catchAsync(async (req: AuthenticatedRequest, res: Response) => {
        if (!req.user) throw new UnauthorizedError();
        const ticketId = Number(req.params.id);
        const scheduleId = Number(req.params.scheduleId);
        if (!ticketId || !scheduleId) throw new BadRequestError('Invalid IDs');
        
        const result = await this.ticketService.linkTicketToSchedule(ticketId, scheduleId, req.user.id);
        res.json(result);
    });

    closeTicketDirectly = catchAsync(async (req: AuthenticatedRequest, res: Response) => {
        if (!req.user) throw new UnauthorizedError();
        const ticketId = Number(req.params.id);
        const { message } = req.body;
        if (!ticketId || !message) throw new BadRequestError('ID and message are required');

        const result = await this.ticketService.closeTicketDirectly(ticketId, message, req.user.id);
        res.json(result);
    });

    markAsExpress = catchAsync(async (req: AuthenticatedRequest, res: Response) => {
        if (!req.user) throw new UnauthorizedError();
        const ticketId = Number(req.params.id);
        if (!ticketId) throw new BadRequestError('Invalid ticket ID');
        const result = await this.ticketService.markAsExpress(ticketId, req.user.id);
        res.json(result);
    });
}


==================================================
START FILE: .\modules\ticket\ticket.repository.ts
==================================================

import { pool } from '../../config/db';
import { QueryRunner } from '../../types';
import { TicketStatus } from '../../types';

export interface TicketFilters {
    status?: string;
    page?: number;
    limit?: number;
}

export class TicketRepository {
    async findAll(db: QueryRunner, filters: TicketFilters = {}): Promise<{ data: any[]; total: number }> {
        const { status = TicketStatus.OPEN, page = 1, limit = 100 } = filters;
        const offset = (page - 1) * limit;

        const baseQuery = `
            FROM tickets t
            LEFT JOIN clients c ON t.client_id = c.id
            LEFT JOIN equipments e ON t."equipmentId" = e.id
            LEFT JOIN profiles p ON t.created_by_user_id = p.id
        `;

        let whereClause = '';
        let params: any[] = [];

        if (status === 'all') {
            whereClause = 'WHERE t.status != $1';
            params = [TicketStatus.DELETED];
        } else if (status === TicketStatus.OPEN) {
            whereClause = 'WHERE t.status IN ($1, $2) AND t."scheduleId" IS NULL';
            params = [TicketStatus.OPEN, 'acknowledged'];
        } else {
            whereClause = 'WHERE t.status = $1';
            params = [status];
        }

        const countRes = await db.query(`SELECT COUNT(*) ${baseQuery} ${whereClause}`, params);
        const total = parseInt(countRes.rows[0].count, 10);

        const { rows } = await db.query(
            `SELECT t.*, c.name as "clientName",
                CONCAT(
                    e.brand, ' ', e.model, 
                    CASE WHEN e."serialNumber" IS NOT NULL THEN CONCAT(' (', e."serialNumber", ')') ELSE '' END,
                    CASE WHEN e.nickname IS NOT NULL AND e.nickname != '' THEN CONCAT(' [', e.nickname, ']') ELSE '' END
                ) as "equipmentInfo",
                p.first_name as "userFirstName", p.last_name as "userLastName"
            ${baseQuery} ${whereClause}
            ORDER BY t."createdAt" DESC
            LIMIT $${params.length + 1} OFFSET $${params.length + 2}`,
            [...params, limit, offset]
        );

        return { data: rows, total };
    }

    async findById(id: number, db: QueryRunner): Promise<any | null> {
        const { rows } = await db.query(`
            SELECT t.*, c.name as "clientName", c.address as "clientAddress", c.nif as "clientNif", 
                c.is_blacklisted, c.blacklist_reason,
                CONCAT(
                    e.brand, ' ', e.model, 
                    CASE WHEN e."serialNumber" IS NOT NULL THEN CONCAT(' (', e."serialNumber", ')') ELSE '' END,
                    CASE WHEN e.nickname IS NOT NULL AND e.nickname != '' THEN CONCAT(' [', e.nickname, ']') ELSE '' END
                ) as "equipmentInfo",
                p.first_name as "userFirstName", p.last_name as "userLastName"
            FROM tickets t
            LEFT JOIN clients c ON t.client_id = c.id
            LEFT JOIN equipments e ON t."equipmentId" = e.id
            LEFT JOIN profiles p ON t.created_by_user_id = p.id
            WHERE t.id = $1
        `, [id]);

        if (rows.length === 0) return null;
        const ticket = rows[0];

        const [attachmentsRes, responsesRes] = await Promise.all([
            db.query('SELECT * FROM ticket_attachments WHERE ticket_id = $1 ORDER BY created_at DESC', [id]),
            db.query(`
                SELECT tr.*, CONCAT(p.first_name, ' ', p.last_name) as "authorName", p.role as "role"
                FROM ticket_responses tr
                LEFT JOIN profiles p ON tr.user_id = p.id
                WHERE tr.ticket_id = $1 ORDER BY tr.created_at DESC
            `, [id]),
        ]);

        ticket.attachments = attachmentsRes.rows;
        ticket.responses = responsesRes.rows;
        return ticket;
    }

    async getStats(db: QueryRunner) {
        const { rows } = await db.query(`
            SELECT
                COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE status IN ('open', 'acknowledged')) as open,
                COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE status = 'scheduled') as scheduled,
                COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE status = 'closed') as closed
            FROM tickets
        `);
        return {
            open: parseInt(rows[0].open, 10),
            scheduled: parseInt(rows[0].scheduled, 10),
            closed: parseInt(rows[0].closed, 10),
        };
    }

    /** Leituras sem transação — usam pool directamente. */
    async findByClientId(clientId: number, options: { page: number; limit: number }) {
        const { page, limit } = options;
        const offset = (page - 1) * limit;

        const [countRes, dataRes] = await Promise.all([
            pool.query('SELECT COUNT(*) FROM tickets WHERE client_id = $1 AND status != $2', [clientId, TicketStatus.DELETED]),
            pool.query(`
                SELECT t.*,
                    CONCAT(
                        e.brand, ' ', e.model, 
                        CASE WHEN e."serialNumber" IS NOT NULL THEN CONCAT(' (', e."serialNumber", ')') ELSE '' END,
                        CASE WHEN e.nickname IS NOT NULL AND e.nickname != '' THEN CONCAT(' [', e.nickname, ']') ELSE '' END
                    ) as "equipmentInfo",
                    s."startDate", s."endDate", s."hasReport",
                    COALESCE(
                        (SELECT r.signature IS NOT NULL AND r.signature != '' FROM reports r WHERE r."scheduleId" = s.id AND r.deleted_at IS NULL LIMIT 1),
                        false
                    ) as "isSigned"
                FROM tickets t
                LEFT JOIN equipments e ON t."equipmentId" = e.id
                LEFT JOIN schedules s ON t."scheduleId" = s.id
                WHERE t.client_id = $1 AND t.status != $2
                ORDER BY t."createdAt" DESC LIMIT $3 OFFSET $4
            `, [clientId, TicketStatus.DELETED, limit, offset]),
        ]);

        return { data: dataRes.rows, total: parseInt(countRes.rows[0].count, 10) };
    }

    async getClientStats(clientId: number) {
        const { rows } = await pool.query(`
            SELECT
                COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE status = 'open') as open,
                COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE status = 'scheduled') as scheduled,
                COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE status = 'closed') as closed,
                COUNT(*) as total
            FROM tickets WHERE client_id = $1 AND status != $2
        `, [clientId, TicketStatus.DELETED]);

        return {
            open: parseInt(rows[0].open, 10),
            scheduled: parseInt(rows[0].scheduled, 10),
            closed: parseInt(rows[0].closed, 10),
            total: parseInt(rows[0].total, 10),
        };
    }
}

import { Router } from 'express';
import { authenticateToken, authorizeRoles } from '../../middlewares/auth.middleware';
import { validate } from '../../middlewares/validate.middleware';
import * as ticketValidation from '../../validations/ticket.validation';
import { UserRole } from '../../constants/enums';
import multer from 'multer';
import { TicketRepository } from './ticket.repository';
import { TicketService } from './ticket.service';
import { TicketController } from './ticket.controller';
import { TicketAttachmentRepository } from './ticketAttachment.repository';
import { TicketAttachmentService } from './ticketAttachment.service';
import { TicketAttachmentController } from './ticketAttachment.controller';

const upload = multer({ storage: multer.memoryStorage() });
const router = Router();

const repo = new TicketRepository();
const service = new TicketService(repo);
const controller = new TicketController(service);

const attRepo = new TicketAttachmentRepository();
const attService = new TicketAttachmentService(attRepo);
const attController = new TicketAttachmentController(attService);

const ALL_ROLES = [UserRole.ADMIN, UserRole.TECHNICIAN, UserRole.OFFICE_STAFF, UserRole.SUPER_ADMIN, UserRole.CLIENT];
const STAFF    = [UserRole.ADMIN, UserRole.TECHNICIAN, UserRole.OFFICE_STAFF, UserRole.SUPER_ADMIN];

/**
 * @swagger
 * tags:
 *   name: Tickets
 *   description: Management of tickets
 */

router.get('/', authenticateToken, authorizeRoles(STAFF), controller.getTickets);
router.get('/:id', authenticateToken, authorizeRoles(ALL_ROLES), validate(ticketValidation.ticketIdSchema), controller.getTicketById);
router.post('/', authenticateToken, authorizeRoles(ALL_ROLES), validate(ticketValidation.createTicketSchema), controller.createTicket);
router.post('/:id/responses', authenticateToken, authorizeRoles(ALL_ROLES), validate(ticketValidation.replyToTicketSchema), controller.replyToTicket);
router.delete('/:id', authenticateToken, authorizeRoles([UserRole.ADMIN, UserRole.SUPER_ADMIN]), validate(ticketValidation.ticketIdSchema), controller.deleteTicket);

router.put('/:id/mark-as-read', authenticateToken, authorizeRoles(STAFF), controller.markTicketAsRead);
router.put('/:id/link-schedule/:scheduleId', authenticateToken, authorizeRoles(STAFF), controller.linkTicketToSchedule);
router.post('/:id/close', authenticateToken, authorizeRoles(STAFF), validate(ticketValidation.replyToTicketSchema), controller.closeTicketDirectly);
router.post('/:id/mark-express', authenticateToken, authorizeRoles(STAFF), validate(ticketValidation.ticketIdSchema), controller.markAsExpress);

// Ticket Attachments
router.get('/:id/attachments', authenticateToken, authorizeRoles(ALL_ROLES), attController.getAttachments);
router.post('/:id/attachments', authenticateToken, authorizeRoles(ALL_ROLES), upload.single('file'), attController.uploadAttachment);
router.delete('/:id/attachments/:attachmentId', authenticateToken, authorizeRoles([UserRole.ADMIN, UserRole.SUPER_ADMIN]), attController.deleteAttachment);
router.delete('/attachments/:attachmentId', authenticateToken, authorizeRoles([UserRole.ADMIN, UserRole.SUPER_ADMIN]), attController.deleteAttachment);

export default router;

// Named export for clientPortal compatibility
export const ticketRepository = repo;

START FILE: .\modules\ticket\ticket.service.ts
==================================================

import { pool, withTransactionAs } from '../../config/db';
import { supabase, ATTACHMENTS_BUCKET } from '../../config/supabase';
import { BadRequestError, NotFoundError, UnauthorizedError } from '../../utils/ApiError';
import { TicketStatus } from '../../types';
import * as ticketService from '../../services/ticketService';
import { TicketRepository, TicketFilters } from './ticket.repository';

export class TicketService {
    constructor(private repo: TicketRepository) {}

    async getTickets(filters: TicketFilters) {
        return this.repo.findAll(pool, filters);
    }

    async getTicketById(id: number) {
        const ticket = await this.repo.findById(id, pool);
        if (!ticket) throw new NotFoundError('Ticket not found');

        const bucket = ATTACHMENTS_BUCKET;
        const enrichedAttachments = await Promise.all(
            (ticket.attachments || []).map(async (att: any) => {
                const { data: signed } = await supabase.storage.from(bucket).createSignedUrl(att.storage_path || '', 3600);
                return { ...att, url: signed?.signedUrl || '' };
            })
        );

        return { ...ticket, attachments: enrichedAttachments };
    }

    async createTicket(data: any, userId: string) {
        return withTransactionAs(userId, (db) => ticketService.createFullTicket(db, data, userId));
    }

    async replyToTicket(ticketId: number, message: string, userId: string) {
        if (!message) throw new BadRequestError('Message is required');
        return withTransactionAs(userId, (db) => ticketService.replyToFullTicket(db, ticketId, userId, message));
    }

    async deleteTicket(ticketId: number, userId: string) {
        return withTransactionAs(userId, async (db) => {
            const { rows, rowCount } = await db.query(
                'UPDATE tickets SET status = $1, "updatedAt" = $2 WHERE id = $3 RETURNING *',
                [TicketStatus.DELETED, new Date().toISOString(), ticketId]
            );
            if (rowCount === 0) throw new NotFoundError('Ticket not found');
            return rows[0];
        });
    }


    async markTicketAsRead(ticketId: number, userId: string) {
        await withTransactionAs(userId, (db) =>
            db.query(
                'UPDATE ticket_responses SET "isNew" = false WHERE ticket_id = $1 AND user_id != $2 AND "isNew" = true',
                [ticketId, userId]
            )
        );
    }

    async linkTicketToSchedule(ticketId: number, scheduleId: number, userId: string) {
        return withTransactionAs(userId, (db) => ticketService.linkTicketToSchedule(db, ticketId, scheduleId, userId));
    }

    async closeTicketDirectly(ticketId: number, message: string, userId: string) {
        return withTransactionAs(userId, (db) => ticketService.closeTicketDirectly(db, ticketId, userId, message));
    }

    async markAsExpress(ticketId: number, userId: string) {
        return withTransactionAs(userId, async (db) => {
            const { rows, rowCount } = await db.query(
                'UPDATE tickets SET status = $1, "updatedAt" = $2 WHERE id = $3 AND status IN ($4, $5) RETURNING *',
                [TicketStatus.SCHEDULED, new Date().toISOString(), ticketId, TicketStatus.OPEN, TicketStatus.ACKNOWLEDGED]
            );
            if (rowCount === 0) throw new NotFoundError('Ticket not found or already processed');
            return rows[0];
        });
    }
}

import { Response } from 'express';
import { AuthenticatedRequest } from '../../middlewares/auth.middleware';
import { catchAsync } from '../../utils/catchAsync';
import { BadRequestError, UnauthorizedError } from '../../utils/ApiError';
import { TicketAttachmentService } from './ticketAttachment.service';

export class TicketAttachmentController {
    constructor(private service: TicketAttachmentService) {}

    getAttachments = catchAsync(async (req: AuthenticatedRequest, res: Response) => {
        const ticketId = Number(req.params.id);
        const result = await this.service.getAttachments(ticketId);
        res.json(result);
    });

    uploadAttachment = catchAsync(async (req: AuthenticatedRequest, res: Response) => {
        if (!req.user) throw new UnauthorizedError();
        const ticketId = Number(req.params.id);
        
        if (!req.file) throw new BadRequestError('No file uploaded.');

        const result = await this.service.uploadAttachment(ticketId, req.file, req.user.id);
        res.status(201).json(result);
    });

    deleteAttachment = catchAsync(async (req: AuthenticatedRequest, res: Response) => {
        if (!req.user) throw new UnauthorizedError();
        const attachmentId = req.params.attachmentId;
        
        await this.service.deleteAttachment(attachmentId, req.user.id);
        res.sendStatus(204);
    });
}

import { QueryRunner } from '../../types';

export class TicketAttachmentRepository {
    async findByTicketId(ticketId: number, db: QueryRunner) {
        const query = 'SELECT * FROM ticket_attachments WHERE ticket_id = $1 ORDER BY created_at DESC';
        const { rows } = await db.query(query, [ticketId]);
        return rows;
    }

    async findById(id: string, db: QueryRunner) {
        const query = 'SELECT * FROM ticket_attachments WHERE id = $1';
        const { rows } = await db.query(query, [id]);
        return rows[0] || null;
    }
}


==================================================
START FILE: .\modules\ticket\ticketAttachment.service.ts
==================================================

import { supabase, ATTACHMENTS_BUCKET } from '../../config/supabase';
import { pool, withTransactionAs } from '../../config/db';
import { ApiError, BadRequestError, NotFoundError } from '../../utils/ApiError';
import { TicketAttachmentRepository } from './ticketAttachment.repository';

export class TicketAttachmentService {
    constructor(private attachmentRepo: TicketAttachmentRepository) {}

    async getAttachments(ticketId: number) {
        const attachments = await this.attachmentRepo.findByTicketId(ticketId, pool);

        const result = await Promise.all((attachments || []).map(async (att: any) => {
            const { data: signed } = await supabase.storage.from(ATTACHMENTS_BUCKET).createSignedUrl(att.storage_path || '', 3600);
            return { ...att, url: signed?.signedUrl || '' };
        }));

        return result;
    }

    async uploadAttachment(ticketId: number, file: Express.Multer.File, userId: string) {
        if (!file) throw new BadRequestError('No file uploaded.');

        const fileExt = file.originalname.split('.').pop();
        const filePath = `tickets/${ticketId}/${Date.now()}-${Math.round(Math.random() * 1E9)}.${fileExt}`;

        const { error: uploadError } = await supabase.storage.from(ATTACHMENTS_BUCKET).upload(filePath, file.buffer, { contentType: file.mimetype });
        if (uploadError) throw new ApiError(500, 'Upload failed', uploadError.message);

        return await withTransactionAs(userId, async (db) => {
            const { rows } = await db.query(
                'INSERT INTO ticket_attachments (ticket_id, file_name, mime_type, storage_path, uploaded_by_user_id) VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5) RETURNING *',
                [ticketId, file.originalname, file.mimetype, filePath, userId]
            );
            return rows[0];
        });
    }

    async deleteAttachment(attachmentId: string, userId: string) {
        await withTransactionAs(userId, async (db) => {
            const att = await this.attachmentRepo.findById(attachmentId, db);

            if (att) {
                await supabase.storage.from(ATTACHMENTS_BUCKET).remove([att.storage_path]);
                const { rowCount } = await db.query('DELETE FROM ticket_attachments WHERE id = $1', [attachmentId]);
                if (rowCount === 0) throw new NotFoundError('Attachment not found.');
            }
        });
    }
}


==================================================
START FILE: .\scripts\add_audit_columns.ts
==================================================

import { pool } from '../config/db';
import { logger } from '../utils/logger';

async function migrate() {
    const client = await pool.connect();
    try {
        logger.info('🚀 Iniciando migração de colunas de auditoria...');

        await client.query('BEGIN');

        // 1. Criar função de trigger para updated_at se não existir
        await client.query(`
            CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION update_updated_at_column()
            RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
            BEGIN
                NEW.updated_at = NOW();
                RETURN NEW;
            END;
            $$ language 'plpgsql';
        `);

        // 2. Tabela: schedules
        logger.info('--- Atualizando tabela: schedules ---');
        await client.query(`
            ALTER TABLE schedules 
            ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
            ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
            ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS created_by UUID REFERENCES profiles(id),
            ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS updated_by UUID REFERENCES profiles(id);
        `);

        await client.query(`
            DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS set_updated_at_schedules ON schedules;
            CREATE TRIGGER set_updated_at_schedules
            BEFORE UPDATE ON schedules
            FOR EACH ROW
            EXECUTE FUNCTION update_updated_at_column();
        `);

        // 3. Tabela: reports
        logger.info('--- Atualizando tabela: reports ---');
        await client.query(`
            ALTER TABLE reports 
            ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
            ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
            ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS updated_by UUID REFERENCES profiles(id);
            -- Nota: created_by já existe como TEXT, vamos tentar converter para UUID se possível ou manter compatibilidade
        `);

        // Verificar o tipo de created_by em reports
        const reportCols = await client.query(`
            SELECT column_name, data_type 
            FROM information_schema.columns 
            WHERE table_name = 'reports' AND column_name = 'created_by'
        `);

        if (reportCols.rows.length > 0 && reportCols.rows[0].data_type === 'text') {
            logger.info('Convertendo reports.created_by de TEXT para UUID...');
            await client.query(`
                ALTER TABLE reports 
                ALTER COLUMN created_by TYPE UUID USING created_by::uuid;
                
                ALTER TABLE reports
                ADD CONSTRAINT reports_created_by_fkey FOREIGN KEY (created_by) REFERENCES profiles(id);
            `);
        }

        await client.query(`
            DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS set_updated_at_reports ON reports;
            CREATE TRIGGER set_updated_at_reports
            BEFORE UPDATE ON reports
            FOR EACH ROW
            EXECUTE FUNCTION update_updated_at_column();
        `);

        // 4. Tabela: internal_tasks
        logger.info('--- Atualizando tabela: internal_tasks ---');
        await client.query(`
            ALTER TABLE internal_tasks 
            ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS updated_by UUID REFERENCES profiles(id);
        `);

        await client.query(`
            DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS set_updated_at_internal_tasks ON internal_tasks;
            CREATE TRIGGER set_updated_at_internal_tasks
            BEFORE UPDATE ON internal_tasks
            FOR EACH ROW
            EXECUTE FUNCTION update_updated_at_column();
        `);

        await client.query('COMMIT');
        logger.info('✅ Migração concluída com sucesso!');

    } catch (err) {
        await client.query('ROLLBACK');
        logger.error({ err }, '❌ Erro na migração');
    } finally {
        client.release();
        process.exit();
    }
}

migrate();

==================================================
START FILE: .\scripts\check_ghost_reservations.ts
==================================================

import { supabase } from '../config/supabase';

async function checkGhostReservations() {
    const { data: parts, error: partsError } = await supabase
        .from('parts')
        .select('id, reference, designation, reserved_quantity, reserved_quantity_foss')
        .or('reserved_quantity.gt.0,reserved_quantity_foss.gt.0');

    if (partsError) {
        console.error('Error fetching parts:', partsError);
        return;
    }

    if (!parts || parts.length === 0) {
        console.log('No parts found with active reservations in the parts table.');
        return;
    }

    console.log(`Found ${parts.length} parts with reservations in the parts table. Checking corresponding schedule_parts...`);

    for (const part of parts) {
        // Direct reservations (not through kits)
        const { data: directRes, error: directError } = await supabase
            .from('schedule_parts')
            .select('quantity, schedules!inner(id, title, isCompleted)')
            .eq('partId', part.id)
            .eq('schedules.isCompleted', false);

        if (directError) {
            console.error(`Error checking direct reservations for part ${part.id}:`, directError);
            continue;
        }

        // Potential kit reservations? (this part is a component of a kit that is reserved)
        const { data: kits, error: kitsError } = await supabase
            .from('part_components')
            .select('parent_part_id, quantity')
            .eq('child_part_id', part.id);

        let kitTotalCount = 0;
        if (kits && kits.length > 0) {
            for (const kit of kits) {
                const { data: kitRes } = await supabase
                    .from('schedule_parts')
                    .select('quantity, schedules!inner(id, title, isCompleted)')
                    .eq('partId', kit.parent_part_id)
                    .eq('schedules.isCompleted', false);

                if (kitRes) {
                    kitRes.forEach(r => {
                        kitTotalCount += r.quantity * kit.quantity;
                    });
                }
            }
        }

        const directCount = directRes?.reduce((acc, r) => acc + r.quantity, 0) || 0;
        const totalExpected = directCount + kitTotalCount;
        const totalInPartsTable = (part.reserved_quantity || 0) + (part.reserved_quantity_foss || 0);

        if (totalInPartsTable !== totalExpected) {
            console.log(`[!] DISCREPANCY for Part ID ${part.id} (${part.reference}):`);
            console.log(`    Parts table says: ${totalInPartsTable}`);
            console.log(`    Schedule parts says: ${totalExpected} (${directCount} direct, ${kitTotalCount} via kits)`);

            // If there's a discrepancy, let's see which schedules are linked to this part even if completed
            const { data: allLinked } = await supabase
                .from('schedule_parts')
                .select('quantity, schedules!inner(id, title, isCompleted)')
                .eq('partId', part.id);

            if (allLinked && allLinked.length > 0) {
                console.log(`    Total linked schedules (even completed): ${allLinked.length}`);
                allLinked.forEach(l => console.log(`      - Sch ${l.schedules.id} (Completed: ${l.schedules.isCompleted}): Qty ${l.quantity}`));
            }
        }
    }
}

checkGhostReservations();

START FILE: .\scripts\check_inconsistencies.ts
==================================================

import { supabase } from '../config/supabase';
import { logger } from '../utils/logger';

async function checkInconsistencies() {
    const { data, error } = await supabase
        .from('schedules')
        .select('id, isCompleted, hasReport, title')
        .eq('hasReport', true)
        .eq('isCompleted', false);

    if (error) {
        console.error('Error fetching schedules:', error);
        return;
    }

    if (data && data.length > 0) {
        console.log(`Found ${data.length} schedules with reports but NOT completed:`);
        data.forEach(s => console.log(`- ID ${s.id}: ${s.title}`));

        // Let's check parts for one of them
        const scheduleId = data[0].id;
        const { data: parts, error: partsError } = await supabase
            .from('schedule_parts')
            .select('partId, quantity, parts(designation, reserved_quantity)')
            .eq('scheduleId', scheduleId);

        if (partsError) {
            console.error('Error fetching parts:', partsError);
        } else {
            console.log(`Parts for Schedule ${scheduleId}:`);
            parts.forEach((p: any) => console.log(`- Part ID ${p.partId} (${p.parts?.designation}): Qty ${p.quantity}, Reserved Total in Part Table: ${p.parts?.reserved_quantity}`));
        }
    } else {
        console.log('No schedules found with reports but NOT completed.');
    }
}

checkInconsistencies();

import { pool } from '../config/db';
import { logger } from '../utils/logger';

async function debugUsers() {
    try {
        console.log("Checking for orphan profiles (profiles without auth.users)...");
        const { rows: orphans } = await pool.query(`
            SELECT p.id, p.email, p.role 
            FROM public.profiles p
            LEFT JOIN auth.users u ON p.id = u.id
            WHERE u.id IS NULL;
        `);

        if (orphans.length > 0) {
            console.log(`Found ${orphans.length} orphan profiles:`);
            orphans.forEach(o => console.log(`- ID: ${o.id}, Email: ${o.email}, Role: ${o.role}`));
        } else {
            console.log("No orphan profiles found.");
        }

        console.log("\nChecking for duplicate emails in profiles...");
        const { rows: duplicates } = await pool.query(`
            SELECT email, count(*) 
            FROM public.profiles 
            GROUP BY email 
            HAVING count(*) > 1;
        `);

        if (duplicates.length > 0) {
            console.log(`Found duplicate emails in profiles:`);
            duplicates.forEach(d => console.log(`- Email: ${d.email}, Count: ${d.count}`));
        } else {
            console.log("No duplicate emails found in profiles.");
        }

    } catch (err) {
        console.error("Error debugging users:", err);
    } finally {
        pool.end();
    }
}

debugUsers();

START FILE: .\scripts\fix_audit.ts
==================================================

import { pool } from '../config/db';

async function fixAuditAndReenable() {
    try {
        console.log("Updating process_audit_log function with robustness...");
        await pool.query(`
            CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.process_audit_log() RETURNS trigger AS $$
            DECLARE
                user_id uuid;
                user_name text;
                claims jsonb;
            BEGIN
                -- Tenta obter o ID do utilizador de várias fontes de forma segura
                -- 1. Variável de sessão personalizada
                BEGIN
                    user_id := NULLIF(current_setting('app.current_user_id', true), '')::uuid;
                EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN
                    user_id := NULL;
                END;

                -- 2. Se ainda for nulo, tenta extrair do JWT do Supabase
                IF user_id IS NULL THEN
                    BEGIN
                        claims := NULLIF(current_setting('request.jwt.claims', true), '')::jsonb;
                        IF claims IS NOT NULL THEN
                            user_id := (claims ->> 'sub')::uuid;
                        END IF;
                    EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN
                        user_id := NULL;
                    END;
                END IF;

                -- Busca o nome do utilizador se o ID estiver disponível e for um UUID válido
                IF user_id IS NOT NULL THEN
                    SELECT concat(first_name, ' ', last_name) INTO user_name
                    FROM profiles
                    WHERE id = user_id;

                    -- Fallback se o perfil não tiver nome ou não for encontrado
                    IF user_name IS NULL OR trim(user_name) = '' THEN
                        user_name := 'Utilizador Desconhecido';
                    END IF;
                ELSE
                    -- Contexto de sistema (ex: Convites via Service Role)
                    user_name := 'SISTEMA / ADMIN (Service Role)';
                END IF;

                -- Gravação do LOG de auditoria
                IF (TG_OP = 'INSERT') THEN
                    INSERT INTO audit_logs (table_name, record_id, operation, old_data, new_data, changed_by, changed_by_name)
                    VALUES (TG_TABLE_NAME, 
                            CASE 
                                WHEN TG_TABLE_NAME IN ('report_parts', 'report_technicians', 'schedule_parts', 'schedule_technicians', 'schedule_time_blocks', 'ticket_responses') THEN NULL 
                                ELSE (to_jsonb(NEW) ->> 'id')::text 
                            END, 
                            'INSERT', NULL, to_jsonb(NEW), user_id, user_name);
                ELSIF (TG_OP = 'UPDATE') THEN
                    INSERT INTO audit_logs (table_name, record_id, operation, old_data, new_data, changed_by, changed_by_name)
                    VALUES (TG_TABLE_NAME, 
                            CASE 
                                WHEN TG_TABLE_NAME IN ('report_parts', 'report_technicians', 'schedule_parts', 'schedule_technicians', 'schedule_time_blocks', 'ticket_responses') THEN NULL 
                                ELSE (to_jsonb(NEW) ->> 'id')::text 
                            END, 
                            'UPDATE', to_jsonb(OLD), to_jsonb(NEW), user_id, user_name);
                ELSIF (TG_OP = 'DELETE') THEN
                    INSERT INTO audit_logs (table_name, record_id, operation, old_data, new_data, changed_by, changed_by_name)
                    VALUES (TG_TABLE_NAME, 
                            CASE 
                                WHEN TG_TABLE_NAME IN ('report_parts', 'report_technicians', 'schedule_parts', 'schedule_technicians', 'schedule_time_blocks', 'ticket_responses') THEN NULL 
                                ELSE (to_jsonb(OLD) ->> 'id')::text 
                            END, 
                            'DELETE', to_jsonb(OLD), NULL, user_id, user_name);
                END IF;

                RETURN NULL;
            EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN
                -- NUNCA bloqueia a operação principal se a auditoria falhar
                -- Apenas retorna NULL (ignora o erro no AFTER trigger)
                RETURN NULL;
            END;
            $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER;
        `);
        console.log("Function process_audit_log updated successfully.");

        console.log("Re-enabling audit trigger on profiles...");
        await pool.query('ALTER TABLE public.profiles ENABLE TRIGGER audit_trigger_on_profiles;');
        console.log("Audit trigger re-enabled.");
    } catch (err) {
        console.error("Error during repair:", err);
    } finally {
        await pool.end();
    }
}

fixAuditAndReenable();

START FILE: .\scripts\fix_notification_defaults.ts

import { pool } from '../config/db';

async function migrate() {
    console.log('🚀 Corrigindo defaults: Telegram desativado por padrão...');

    const client = await pool.connect();
    try {
        await client.query('BEGIN');

        // 1. Atualizar o DEFAULT da coluna
        await client.query(`
            ALTER TABLE profiles 
            ALTER COLUMN notification_prefs SET DEFAULT '{
                "new_schedule": { "email": true, "telegram": false },
                "new_report": { "email": true, "telegram": false },
                "ticket_reply": { "email": true, "telegram": false },
                "docs_uploaded": { "email": true, "telegram": false }
            }'::JSONB;
        `);

        // 2. Atualizar registos existentes onde o telegram está true mas não há chatid
        console.log('--- Removendo ativação de Telegram em perfis sem vínculo...');
        await client.query(`
            UPDATE profiles 
            SET notification_prefs = jsonb_set(
                jsonb_set(
                    jsonb_set(
                        jsonb_set(
                            notification_prefs, 
                            '{new_schedule,telegram}', 'false'
                        ), 
                        '{new_report,telegram}', 'false'
                    ), 
                    '{ticket_reply,telegram}', 'false'
                ), 
                '{docs_uploaded,telegram}', 'false'
            )
            WHERE telegramchatid IS NULL OR telegramchatid = '';
        `);

        await client.query('COMMIT');
        console.log('✅ Defaults corrigidos com sucesso!');
    } catch (error) {
        await client.query('ROLLBACK');
        console.log('❌ Erro na correção:', error);
        process.exit(1);
    } finally {
        client.release();
    }
}

migrate();

START FILE: .\scripts\migrate_client_users.ts
==================================================

import { pool } from '../config/db';
import { logger } from '../utils/logger';

async function migrateClientUsers() {
    const client = await pool.connect();
    try {
        await client.query('BEGIN');

        logger.info('Creating client_users table...');
        await client.query(`
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.client_users (
                id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
                user_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES public.profiles(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
                client_id BIGINT NOT NULL REFERENCES public.clients(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
                created_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE DEFAULT NOW()
            );
        `);

        // Create a unique constraint to avoid duplicates
        await client.query(`
            CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS client_users_user_client_idx
            ON public.client_users (user_id, client_id);
        `);

        logger.info('Migrating existing client_ids from profiles to client_users...');

        // Migrate where client_id is not null and the pair doesn't exist yet
        await client.query(`
            INSERT INTO public.client_users (user_id, client_id)
            SELECT id, client_id
            FROM public.profiles
            WHERE client_id IS NOT NULL
            ON CONFLICT (user_id, client_id) DO NOTHING;
        `);

        // Optionally setup RLS (Row Level Security) if needed later
        // We ensure RLS is enabled if the project requires it
        await client.query(`
            ALTER TABLE public.client_users ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
        `);

        // Grants
        await client.query(`
            GRANT ALL ON TABLE public.client_users TO authenticated;
            GRANT ALL ON TABLE public.client_users TO service_role;
            GRANT SELECT ON TABLE public.client_users TO anon;
        `);

        await client.query('COMMIT');
        logger.info('Migration completed successfully.');
    } catch (error) {
        await client.query('ROLLBACK');
        logger.error({ error }, 'Error during migration. Rolled back.');
    } finally {
        client.release();
        process.exit(0);
    }
}

migrateClientUsers();

import { pool } from '../config/db';
import { logger } from '../utils/logger';

async function runMigration() {
    const client = await pool.connect();
    try {
        logger.info('Starting migration: Create auth_audit_logs table');
        
        await client.query(`
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS auth_audit_logs (
                id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
                timestamp TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                email TEXT NOT NULL,
                status TEXT NOT NULL, -- 'success' or 'failure'
                ip TEXT,
                user_agent TEXT,
                reason TEXT, -- error message if failure
                user_id UUID, -- reference to profiles(id) if success
                created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
            );
        `);

        await client.query('CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_auth_audit_logs_timestamp ON auth_audit_logs(timestamp DESC);');
        await client.query('CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_auth_audit_logs_email ON auth_audit_logs(email);');
        await client.query('CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_auth_audit_logs_status ON auth_audit_logs(status);');
        
        logger.info('Migration completed successfully');
    } catch (error) {
        logger.error(error, 'Error during migration:');
        process.exit(1);
    } finally {
        client.release();
        process.exit(0);
    }
}

runMigration();


==================================================
START FILE: .\scripts\migration_equipment_ownership.ts
==================================================

import { pool } from '../config/db';

async function migrate() {
    console.log('🚀 Iniciando migração: Ownership History e Status Inativo...');

    const client = await pool.connect();
    try {
        await client.query('BEGIN');

        // 1. Criar tabela de Propriedade
        console.log('--- Criando tabela equipment_ownership...');
        await client.query(`
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS equipment_ownership (
                id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
                equipment_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES equipments(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
                client_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES clients(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
                start_date TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
                end_date TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE,
                created_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE DEFAULT NOW()
            );
        `);

        // 2. Adicionar coluna status em equipments
        console.log('--- Adicionando coluna status em equipments...');
        // Verificamos se a coluna já existe para evitar erro em re-run
        const checkColumn = await client.query(`
            SELECT column_name 
            FROM information_schema.columns 
            WHERE table_name='equipments' AND column_name='status'
        `);
        
        if (checkColumn.rowCount === 0) {
            await client.query(`
                ALTER TABLE equipments 
                ADD COLUMN status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'active';
            `);
        }

        // 3. Migrar donos atuais para a nova tabela
        console.log('--- Migrando proprietários atuais...');
        // Apenas para equipamentos que ainda não têm registo na ownership
        await client.query(`
            INSERT INTO equipment_ownership (equipment_id, client_id, start_date)
            SELECT id, "clientId", NOW()
            FROM equipments e
            WHERE NOT EXISTS (
                SELECT 1 FROM equipment_ownership eo WHERE eo.equipment_id = e.id
            );
        `);

        await client.query('COMMIT');
        console.log('✅ Migração concluída com sucesso!');
    } catch (error) {
        await client.query('ROLLBACK');
        console.log('❌ Erro na migração:', error);
        process.exit(1);
    } finally {
        client.release();
    }
}

migrate();


==================================================
START FILE: .\scripts\migration_fix_pks.ts
==================================================

import { pool } from '../config/db';
import { logger } from '../utils/logger';

async function migrate() {
    const client = await pool.connect();
    try {
        logger.info('Starting migration: Expanding Primary Keys for Virtual Parts');
        await client.query('BEGIN');

        // --- schedule_parts ---
        logger.info('Updating schedule_parts PK...');
        
        // 1. Drop existing PK
        await client.query('ALTER TABLE schedule_parts DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS schedule_parts_pkey');
        
        // 2. Ensure designation is not null
        await client.query("UPDATE schedule_parts SET designation = '' WHERE designation IS NULL");
        await client.query('ALTER TABLE schedule_parts ALTER COLUMN designation SET NOT NULL');
        await client.query("ALTER TABLE schedule_parts ALTER COLUMN designation SET DEFAULT ''");
        
        // 3. Add new PK
        await client.query('ALTER TABLE schedule_parts ADD PRIMARY KEY ("scheduleId", "partId", designation)');

        // --- report_parts ---
        logger.info('Updating report_parts PK...');
        
        // Try to find the constraint name for report_parts (usually it's report_parts_pkey)
        await client.query('ALTER TABLE report_parts DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS report_parts_pkey');
        
        // Ensure designation is not null
        await client.query("UPDATE report_parts SET designation = '' WHERE designation IS NULL");
        await client.query('ALTER TABLE report_parts ALTER COLUMN designation SET NOT NULL');
        await client.query("ALTER TABLE report_parts ALTER COLUMN designation SET DEFAULT ''");
        
        // Add new PK
        await client.query('ALTER TABLE report_parts ADD PRIMARY KEY ("reportId", "partId", designation)');

        await client.query('COMMIT');
        logger.info('Migration completed successfully');
        process.exit(0);
    } catch (error) {
        await client.query('ROLLBACK');
        logger.error(error, 'Migration failed');
        process.exit(1);
    } finally {
        client.release();
    }
}

migrate();

import { pool } from '../config/db';

async function migrate() {
    console.log('🚀 Injetando templates de email configuráveis...');

    const client = await pool.connect();
    try {
        await client.query('BEGIN');

        // 1. Obter templates atuais
        const { rows } = await client.query("SELECT value FROM settings WHERE key = 'email_templates'");
        let templates = rows[0]?.value || {};
        if (typeof templates === 'string') templates = JSON.parse(templates);

        // 2. Definir novos templates
        const newTemplates = {
            ticket_reply: {
                name: "Nova Resposta em Ticket",

</div>`,
                from: "'Suporte Project1' <suporte@microatomo.pt>"
            },
            new_schedule: {
                name: "Novo Agendamento",
                subject: "Novo Agendamento de Assistência: {{scheduleTitle}}",
                body: `<div style="font-family: sans-serif; line-height: 1.5;">
    <h3>Olá,</h3>
    <p>Informamos que foi agendada uma intervenção técnica para o seu equipamento.</p>
    <hr />
    <p><b>Serviço:</b> {{serviceType}}</p>
    <p><b>Equipamento:</b> {{equipmentInfo}}</p>
    <p><b>Início:</b> {{startDate}}</p>
    <p><b>Fim:</b> {{endDate}}</p>

    <p><a href="{{clientUrl}}/schedules" style="display: inline-block; padding: 10px 20px; background-color: #0d6efd; color: white; text-decoration: none; border-radius: 5px;">Ver Agenda no Portal</a></p>
</div>`,
                from: "'Agendamento Project1' <agendamento@microatomo.pt>"
            },
            new_report: {
                name: "Relatório de Serviço Disponível",
                subject: "Relatório Técnico Disponível: #{{reportNumber}}",
                body: `<div style="font-family: sans-serif; line-height: 1.5;">
    <h3>Olá,</h3>
    <p>O relatório técnico da intervenção realizada no seu equipamento <b>{{equipInfo}}</b> já se encontra disponível no portal.</p>
    <p><b>Número do Relatório:</b> {{reportNumber}}</p>
    <hr />
    <p><a href="{{clientUrl}}/reports/{{reportId}}" style="display: inline-block; padding: 10px 20px; background-color: #198754; color: white; text-decoration: none; border-radius: 5px;">Ver Relatório Completo</a></p>
</div>`,
                from: "'Relatórios Project1' <relatorios@microatomo.pt>"
            }
        };

        // 3. Mesclar (preservando existentes se já houver alterações manuais - embora aqui o ideal seja garantir os novos)
        const updatedTemplates = { ...templates, ...newTemplates };

        // 4. Salvar de volta
        await client.query(
            "UPDATE settings SET value = $1 WHERE key = 'email_templates'",
            [JSON.stringify(updatedTemplates)]
        );

        await client.query('COMMIT');
        console.log('✅ Templates de email injetados com sucesso!');
    } catch (error) {
        await client.query('ROLLBACK');
        console.log('❌ Erro na migração de templates:', error);
        process.exit(1);
    } finally {
        client.release();
    }
}

import { pool } from '../config/db';

async function migrate() {
    console.log('🚀 Iniciando migração: Configurações de Notificação (notification_prefs)...');

    const client = await pool.connect();
    try {
        await client.query('BEGIN');

        // 1. Adicionar coluna notification_prefs em profiles
        console.log('--- Adicionando coluna notification_prefs em profiles...');
        const checkColumn = await client.query(`
            SELECT column_name 
            FROM information_schema.columns 
            WHERE table_name='profiles' AND column_name='notification_prefs'
        `);
        
        if (checkColumn.rowCount === 0) {
            await client.query(`
                ALTER TABLE profiles 
                ADD COLUMN notification_prefs JSONB DEFAULT '{
                    "new_schedule": { "email": true, "telegram": true },
                    "new_report": { "email": true, "telegram": true },
                    "ticket_reply": { "email": true, "telegram": true },
                    "docs_uploaded": { "email": true, "telegram": true }
                }'::JSONB;
            `);
            console.log('✅ Coluna notification_prefs adicionada.');
        } else {
            console.log('ℹ️ Coluna notification_prefs já existe.');
        }

        // 2. Garantir que utilizadores existentes (especialmente clientes) têm o objeto preenchido
        // Nota: O DEFAULT tratará de novos, mas para existentes que possam ter NULL (se a coluna já existisse e fosse alterada)
        console.log('--- Atualizando utilizadores existentes com valores padrão...');
        await client.query(`
            UPDATE profiles 
            SET notification_prefs = '{
                "new_schedule": { "email": true, "telegram": true },
                "new_report": { "email": true, "telegram": true },
                "ticket_reply": { "email": true, "telegram": true },
                "docs_uploaded": { "email": true, "telegram": true }
            }'::JSONB
            WHERE notification_prefs IS NULL;
        `);

        await client.query('COMMIT');
        console.log('✅ Migração concluída com sucesso!');
    } catch (error) {
        await client.query('ROLLBACK');
        console.log('❌ Erro na migração:', error);
        process.exit(1);
    } finally {
        client.release();
    }
}

migrate();

import { pool } from '../config/db';
import { logger } from '../utils/logger';

async function migrate() {
    try {
        logger.info('Starting migration: Virtual Parts (TEX)');

        // 1. Add track_stock column to parts
        await pool.query('ALTER TABLE parts ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS track_stock BOOLEAN DEFAULT true');
        
        // 2. Add designation column to link tables
        await pool.query('ALTER TABLE report_parts ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS designation TEXT');
        await pool.query('ALTER TABLE schedule_parts ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS designation TEXT');

        // 3. Insert or Update "TEX" part
        const { rows } = await pool.query('SELECT id FROM parts WHERE reference = $1', ['TEX']);
        if (rows.length === 0) {
            logger.info('Inserting "TEX" placeholder part');
            await pool.query(
                `INSERT INTO parts (reference, designation, track_stock, stock_quantity, reserved_quantity, ordered_quantity, price) 
                 VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6, $7)`,
                ['TEX', 'TEXTO LIVRE', false, 0, 0, 0, 0]
            );
        } else {
            logger.info('"TEX" part already exists, updating track_stock to false');
            await pool.query('UPDATE parts SET track_stock = $1 WHERE reference = $2', [false, 'TEX']);
            // WAIT, fixed the typo in SQL above in my thought process, let me fix it in the CodeContent.
        }

        logger.info('Migration completed successfully');
        process.exit(0);
    } catch (error) {
        logger.error(error, 'Migration failed');
        process.exit(1);
    }
}

==================================================
START FILE: .\scripts\patch_trigger.ts
==================================================

import { pool } from '../config/db';

async function patchTrigger() {
    try {
        console.log("Patching handle_new_user trigger...");
        await pool.query(`
            CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.handle_new_user() RETURNS trigger AS $$
            BEGIN
              -- Usamos ON CONFLICT (email) para permitir convidar utilizadores que possam ter perfis órfãos
              -- E fazemos cast via numeric para evitar erros de cast de integer (ex: "13.0")
              INSERT INTO public.profiles (id, email, role, first_name, last_name, client_id, client_role)
              VALUES (
                new.id,
                new.email,
                new.raw_user_meta_data->>'role',
                new.raw_user_meta_data->>'first_name',
                new.raw_user_meta_data->>'last_name',
                (NULLIF(new.raw_user_meta_data->>'client_id', ''))::numeric::integer,
                new.raw_user_meta_data->>'client_role'
              )
              ON CONFLICT (email) DO UPDATE SET
                id = EXCLUDED.id,
                role = EXCLUDED.role,
                first_name = EXCLUDED.first_name,
                last_name = EXCLUDED.last_name,
                client_id = EXCLUDED.client_id,
                client_role = EXCLUDED.client_role,
                updated_at = now();
              RETURN new;
            EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN
              -- Se falhar, deixamos log para debug mas não bloqueamos o utilizador se for crítico?
              -- Na verdade, se o profile falhar, o utilizador no auth fica sem perfil.
              -- Vamos deixar rebentar para saber o erro exacto nos logs do supabase por agora,
              -- mas com o cast mais robusto.
              RAISE EXCEPTION 'Erro no trigger handle_new_user: %, SQLSTATE: %', SQLERRM, SQLSTATE;
            END;
            $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER;
        `);
        console.log("Trigger patched successfully.");
    } catch (err) {
        console.error("Error patching trigger:", err);
    } finally {
        await pool.end();
    }
}

patchTrigger();


==================================================
START FILE: .\scripts\patch_trigger_and_sync.ts
==================================================

import { pool } from '../config/db';

async function patchAndSync() {
    try {
        console.log("Patching handle_new_user trigger to also insert into client_users...");
        await pool.query(`
            CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.handle_new_user() RETURNS trigger AS $$
            DECLARE
              extracted_client_id integer;
            BEGIN
              extracted_client_id := (NULLIF(new.raw_user_meta_data->>'client_id', ''))::numeric::integer;

              INSERT INTO public.profiles (id, email, role, first_name, last_name, client_id, client_role)
              VALUES (
                new.id,
                new.email,
                new.raw_user_meta_data->>'role',
                new.raw_user_meta_data->>'first_name',
                new.raw_user_meta_data->>'last_name',
                extracted_client_id,
                new.raw_user_meta_data->>'client_role'
              )
              ON CONFLICT (email) DO UPDATE SET
                id = EXCLUDED.id,
                role = EXCLUDED.role,
                first_name = EXCLUDED.first_name,
                last_name = EXCLUDED.last_name,
                client_id = EXCLUDED.client_id,
                client_role = EXCLUDED.client_role,
                updated_at = now();

              IF extracted_client_id IS NOT NULL THEN
                  IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.client_users WHERE user_id = new.id AND client_id = extracted_client_id) THEN
                      INSERT INTO public.client_users (user_id, client_id) VALUES (new.id, extracted_client_id);
                  END IF;
              END IF;

              RETURN new;
            EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN
              RAISE EXCEPTION 'Erro no trigger handle_new_user: %, SQLSTATE: %', SQLERRM, SQLSTATE;
            END;
            $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER;
        `);
        console.log("Trigger patched successfully.");

        console.log("Syncing existing profiles missing in client_users...");
        const res = await pool.query(`
            INSERT INTO public.client_users (user_id, client_id)
            SELECT id, client_id
            FROM public.profiles
            WHERE client_id IS NOT NULL AND client_id > 0
            AND NOT EXISTS (
                SELECT 1 FROM public.client_users cu WHERE cu.user_id = profiles.id AND cu.client_id = profiles.client_id
            );
        `);
        console.log(`Synced ${res.rowCount} missing associations.`);

    } catch (err) {
        console.error("Error patching trigger or syncing:", err);
    } finally {
        await pool.end();
    }
}

patchAndSync();

START FILE: .\scripts\recalculateReservations.ts
==================================================

import { supabase } from '../config/supabase';
import * as inventoryService from '../services/inventoryService';
import { StockType } from '../types';
import { pool } from '../config/db';
import { logger } from '../utils/logger';

async function recalculateReservations() {
    logger.info('[INV_RECALC] Iniciando recalculo de reservas de inventário...');
    const client = await pool.connect();

    try {
        // 1. Resetar todas as reservas na tabela 'parts' para 0
        logger.info('[INV_RECALC] Step 1: Resetando todas as reservas para zero...');
        const { error: resetError } = await supabase
            .from('parts')
            .update({
                reserved_quantity: 0,
                reserved_quantity_contract: 0
            })
            .gte('id', 0); // Seleciona todos

        if (resetError) throw resetError;

        // 2. Obter todas as peças de agendamentos que NÃO estão concluídos
        logger.info('[INV_RECALC] Step 2: Procurando peças em agendamentos abertos...');
        const { data: activeScheduleParts, error: fetchError } = await supabase
            .from('schedule_parts')
            .select(`
                partId,
                quantity,
                stock_type,
                schedules!inner(isCompleted)
            `)
            .eq('schedules.isCompleted', false);

        if (fetchError) throw fetchError;

        logger.info({ count: activeScheduleParts?.length || 0 }, '[INV_RECALC] Linhas de peças para processar.');

        // 3. Aplicar as reservas para cada peça encontrada
        if (activeScheduleParts) {
            let processed = 0;
            for (const sp of activeScheduleParts) {
                await inventoryService.updatePartReservation(
                    client,
                    sp.partId,
                    Number(sp.quantity),
                    (sp.stock_type as StockType) || StockType.GENERAL
                );
                processed++;
                if (processed % 10 === 0) {
                    logger.debug({ progress: `${processed}/${activeScheduleParts.length}` }, '[INV_RECALC] Processando...');
                }
            }
        }

        logger.info('[INV_RECALC] ✅ Sucesso! Todas as reservas foram recalculadas com base nos agendamentos ativos.');
    } catch (error) {
        logger.error(error, '[INV_RECALC] ❌ Erro durante o recalculo');
    } finally {
        client.release();
        process.exit(0);
    }
}

recalculateReservations();

import { Pool } from 'pg';
import dotenv from 'dotenv';

// Carregar variáveis do .env na pasta raiz do server

if (!connectionString) {
    console.error('ERRO: DATABASE_URL não encontrada. Certifica-te que estás na pasta raiz do server e que o ficheiro .env existe.');
    process.exit(1);
}

const pool = new Pool({
    connectionString,
    ssl: {
        rejectUnauthorized: false
    }
});

async function resetImagePaths() {
    process.stdout.write('🔄 A fazer reset da coluna image_path na tabela parts... ');

            process.stdout.write(`✅ SUCESSO! ${result.rowCount} itens atualizados.\n`);
        } finally {
            client.release();
        }
    } catch (err: any) {
        process.stdout.write(`❌ ERRO: ${err.message}\n`);
    } finally {

    }
}

START FILE: .\scripts\sync_all_inventory.ts
==================================================

import * as dotenv from 'dotenv';
dotenv.config();
import { Pool } from 'pg';
import * as inventoryService from '../services/inventoryService';

const pool = new Pool({
    connectionString: process.env.DATABASE_URL || 'missing_db_url'
});

async function main() {
    const db = await pool.connect();
    try {
        const { rows: parts } = await db.query('SELECT id, reference, designation FROM parts');
        console.log(`Starting full inventory reservation sync for ${parts.length} parts...`);

        let fixedCount = 0;
        for (const part of parts) {
            const { rows: before } = await db.query('SELECT reserved_quantity, reserved_quantity_foss FROM parts WHERE id = $1', [part.id]);
            await inventoryService.syncPartStock(db, part.id);
            const { rows: after } = await db.query('SELECT reserved_quantity, reserved_quantity_foss FROM parts WHERE id = $1', [part.id]);

            if (before[0].reserved_quantity !== after[0].reserved_quantity || before[0].reserved_quantity_foss !== after[0].reserved_quantity_foss) {
                console.log(`[FIXED] Part ${part.id} (${part.reference} - ${part.designation}): ` +
                    `${before[0].reserved_quantity}/${before[0].reserved_quantity_foss} -> ` +
                    `${after[0].reserved_quantity}/${after[0].reserved_quantity_foss}`);
                fixedCount++;
            }
        }
        console.log(`\nInventory sync completed! Fixed ${fixedCount} discrepancies.`);
    } catch (err: any) {
        console.error('Error during full sync:', err.message);
    } finally {
        db.release();
        await pool.end();
    }
}

main().catch(console.error);

import { pool, withTransaction } from '../config/db';

async function testAuditInjection() {
    const fakeReq = {
        user: { id: 'cad84179-7649-42f4-9730-74434b873bd4' }
    };

    try {
        console.log('--- TESTING WITH TRANSACTION ---');
        await withTransaction(fakeReq as any, async (db) => {
            const { rows } = await db.query("SELECT current_setting('app.current_user_id', true) as user_id");
            console.log('Session user_id:', rows[0].user_id);

            console.log('Testing insert into parts...');
            const { rows: partRows } = await db.query(
                "INSERT INTO parts (reference, designation, stock_quantity) VALUES ($1, $2, $3) RETURNING id",
                ['TEST-AUDIT-' + Date.now(), 'Audit Test Part', 1]
            );
            const partId = partRows[0].id;
            console.log('Part created:', partId);

            console.log('Checking audit_logs...');
            const { rows: auditRows } = await db.query(
                "SELECT id, operation, table_name, record_id, changed_by, changed_by_name FROM audit_logs WHERE table_name = 'parts' AND record_id = $1 ORDER BY changed_at DESC LIMIT 1",
                [partId.toString()]
            );
            console.log('Audit Log Entry:');
            console.table(auditRows);

            // Rollback to keep DB clean
            throw new Error('ROLLBACK_TEST');
        });
    } catch (err: any) {
        if (err.message === 'ROLLBACK_TEST') {
            console.log('Test completed and rolled back successfully.');
        } else {
            console.error('Test failed:', err);
        }
    } finally {
        process.exit();
    }
}

testAuditInjection();

import { supabase } from '../config/supabase';
import { UserRole } from '../constants/enums';

async function testInvite() {
    const testEmail = `test_invite_${Date.now()}@example.com`;
    console.log(`Testing invite for: ${testEmail}`);

    const inviteData = {
        role: UserRole.CLIENT,
        first_name: "Test",
        last_name: "Invite",
        client_id: 13, // Usando um ID que sabemos que existe (visto no log)
        must_set_password: true
    };

    const { data, error } = await supabase.auth.admin.inviteUserByEmail(testEmail, {
        data: inviteData
    });

    if (error) {
        console.error("FAILED TO INVITE:");
        console.error(JSON.stringify(error, null, 2));
    } else {
        console.log("INVITE SUCCESSFUL:");
        console.log(data);

        // Clean up
        console.log("Cleaning up test user...");
        await supabase.auth.admin.deleteUser(data.user.id);
        console.log("Cleaned up.");
    }
}

import * as dotenv from 'dotenv';
dotenv.config();
import { Pool } from 'pg';

const pool = new Pool({
    connectionString: process.env.DATABASE_URL || 'missing_db_url'
});

async function testSync() {
    console.log('Using DB URL:', process.env.DATABASE_URL?.substring(0, 20) + '...');
    const partId = 87; // Part 87 (60087625) with discrepancy
    const db = await pool.connect();
    try {
        await db.query('BEGIN');

        const { rows: before } = await db.query('SELECT designation, reserved_quantity, reserved_quantity_foss FROM parts WHERE id = $1', [partId]);
        console.log('Before Sync:', before[0]);

        await inventoryService.syncPartStock(db, partId);

        const { rows: after } = await db.query('SELECT designation, reserved_quantity, reserved_quantity_foss FROM parts WHERE id = $1', [partId]);
        console.log('After Sync:', after[0]);

        await db.query('ROLLBACK'); // Rollback so we don't mess up live DB if it's production-like
    } catch (err: any) {
        console.error('Error during testSync:', err.message);
    } finally {
        db.release();
        await pool.end();
    }
}
